**Rolling window:** 2026-09-26 through 2026-10-02

## Best date plans

### 2026-09-26 — Brooklyn Book Festival Children's Day + Grimm Tavern

**Brooklyn Book Festival Children's Day, then a meal.** brewery tavern at Grimm Tavern. It is 1.4 miles from the venue, so budget a subway stop between the two. It starts at 10 am, which leaves the evening for the table.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=209+Joralemon+St%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY+11201&destination=Prospect+Heights%2C+Brooklyn%2C+NY&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Restaurant](https://www.grimmales.com/)

### 2026-10-02 — Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi + Yo-Yo Ma and Gustavo Dudamel

**Dinner, then Yo-Yo Ma and Gustavo Dudamel.** Afro-Caribbean American at Tatiana by Kwame Onwuachi. It is a 1-minute walk from the venue. Yo-Yo Ma and Gustavo Dudamel starts at 7:30 pm, so book the table early.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&destination=10+Lincoln+Center+Plaza%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10023&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://www.lincolncenter.org/venue/david-geffen-hall) · [Event booking](https://www.songkick.com/concerts/43357316-yoyo-ma-at-david-geffen-hall-lincoln-center) · [Restaurant](https://www.tatiananyc.com/)

### 2026-09-26 — Cospa Ramen + Rooftop Cinema Club Midtown: Ocean's 8

**Dinner, then Rooftop Cinema Club Midtown: Ocean's 8.** ramen at Cospa Ramen. It is a 15-minute walk from the venue. Doors 6:15 pm — eat first.
[Walking directions](https://www.google.com/maps/dir/?api=1&origin=Flatiron%2C+New+York%2C+NY&destination=60+W+37th+St%2C+3rd+Floor%2C+New+York%2C+NY+10018&travelmode=walking) · [Event](https://rooftopcinemaclub.com/us/new-york/midtown/screenings/oceans-8-24153)

## What to do this week

### 2026-09-26

#### [Brooklyn Book Festival Children's Day](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/)

10:00 AM · books · Free · Brooklyn Borough Hall and Brooklyn Heights Library · Downtown Brooklyn · Brooklyn · Score 73.0  
Brooklyn Book Festival's Children's Day brings author readings, workshops, performances, signings, and art projects to indoor Downtown Brooklyn venues after weather changes.
**Timing:** Children's Day is listed for 10am-4pm; outdoor market moved indoors because of the nor'easter forecast.
**Practical note:** Festival announced outdoor-stage changes because of dangerous wind and rain; check the latest venue moves before leaving.
**Why now:** Children's Day falls on the first day of the window and was updated for storm conditions.
**On-sale watch:** Weather moved outdoor programming indoors.
[Official](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Tickets / booking](https://brooklynbookfestival.org/) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://www.theskint.com/ongoing-events/)

#### [Bollywood and Bhangra at Pier 46](https://hudsonriverpark.org/visit/events/event/bollywood-and-bhangra-september-12-2026/)

10:00 AM · outdoors · Free · Hudson River Park Pier 46 · West Village · Manhattan · Score 70.0  
Ajna Dance Company leads an all-levels outdoor class in Bollywood and Bhangra technique, rhythm, expression, and high-energy movement at Hudson River Park.
**Timing:** Saturday mornings in September and October, 10-11am.
**Practical note:** Bring water and a mat; NWS forecasts rain and wind Saturday, so verify before heading to the pier.
**Why now:** Part of Hudson River Park's September-October Saturday morning series.
**On-sale watch:** Advance booking is recommended.

#### [Last Saturdays: Open Classroom](https://www.green-wood.com/event/last-saturdays-open-classroom-4/)

12:00 PM · workshop · Free · The Green-House at Green-Wood · Greenwood Heights · Brooklyn · Score 69.0  
Green-Wood opens its classroom for hands-on activities; this edition uses flowers harvested from Jean Shin's Offering installation to make paint pigments and create flower-color-inspired paintings.
**Timing:** Drop in 12-3pm; registration recommended for headcount.
**Practical note:** Indoor classroom program; Green-Wood recommends registration, and Saturday travel may be wet and windy.
**Why now:** This is the last-Saturday September edition of the monthly open classroom.
**On-sale watch:** Registration is recommended for planning but drop-ins are allowed.

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 56.0  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.

#### [Bryant Park Birding Tour](https://bryantpark.org/activities/birding-tours)

8:00 AM · outdoors · Free · Bryant Park · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 48.0  
Environmental educator Gabriel Willow leads a free fall-migration walk looking for warblers, tanagers, vireos, thrushes, and other birds passing through Midtown.
**Timing:** Tuesdays at 8am during migration season; meet at the info kiosk on 6th Ave across from 41st St.
**Practical note:** Mostly cloudy weather is expected to improve after early-week rain; bring binoculars if you have them.
**Why now:** Fall migration tours run Tuesdays, Thursdays, and Fridays through October 10.
[Official](https://bryantpark.org/activities/birding-tours) · [Tickets / booking](https://bryantpark.org/activities/birding-tours) · [Discovered via The Skint](https://www.theskint.com/ongoing-events/)

5:00 PM · outdoors · Free · Bryant Park · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 48.0  
A free guided walk with Gabriel Willow spotting migratory birds in Bryant Park, a Midtown hotspot for warblers, tanagers, vireos, thrushes, and other fall visitors.

**Why now:** Fall migration tours run on Thursday evenings through October 10.

4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 49.0  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

#### [Bryant Park Birding Tour](https://bryantpark.org/activities/birding-tours)

5:00 PM · outdoors · Free · Bryant Park · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 48.0  
Free Friday fall-migration birding walk led by Gabriel Willow in Bryant Park, with beginner and experienced birders welcome.
**Timing:** Fridays at 5pm during migration season; meet at the info kiosk on 6th Ave across from 41st St.
**Practical note:** Partly sunny and mild conditions are forecast; bring binoculars and expect a short outdoor walk.
**Why now:** Friday evening tour falls in the active migration-season schedule.
